# Non-8H completeness unknowns (v1.23)

Observed official settlements are taken from already-saved official monthly
ZIPs (raw bytes) where a ZIP exists for the inventory month, and from the
already-saved 2026-11 REST parsed-JSON files otherwise. The frozen DB and
delta CSVs were not used as the official month source (they remain in the
source register as library inputs). No official file was refetched. No
zero-fill and no interpolation were applied.

**No official settlement-schedule source has been used.** Every expected-count
denominator that can be written down from these data is an *observed-assumption*
only: a constant interval that happens to span a full CST month. It is
inferred, not officially verified. Months that are not a full-month constant
interval (interval change, internal gaps, mid-month listing/delisting or a
shorter observed span, research-window truncation, irregular gaps, or too few
points) are **not determinable** from the saved data alone.

The research window is 2023-01-01T00:00:00Z to 2026-11-26T12:00:00Z inclusive.
CST months are UTC+8. `complete=true` in inventory r5 means passed per that
batch's source and comparison scope only, not a full-period or cross-frequency
verification. CST vs UTC month-boundary effects are flagged in dedicated
columns (see below; 復盤 Issue #1 comment 7239320542). 2026-11 is a partial
month with no month-end crossing in this window.

The full per-row listing is in `nonh8_completeness_unknowns_v1_23.csv`.
Self-run scans are team execution evidence, not ChatGPT runtime certification.

FIB-4H-LONG-V3 remains NO-GO. live_approved is false.

## CST vs UTC month-boundary effects

Funding completeness in this research is bucketed by CST (UTC+8) month; performance month buckets are UTC months, so boundaries can misalign (復盤 Issue #1 comment 7239320542). A settlement whose UTC timestamp is on or after 16:00Z on the last UTC day of a month falls in the **next CST month** and in the **current UTC month**. These crossings are counted *one by one* from saved official timestamps (not by formula). Typologically an 8H contract has exactly one such slot per month (16:00Z); 4H and 1H contracts may have several in the last 8 hours (4H: 16:00Z and 20:00Z; 1H: 16:00Z through 23:00Z).

The research window ends 2026-11-26T12:00:00Z, so CST 2026-11 has **no month-end crossing** in the saved data and is labelled a partial month. Incoming opening crossings from 31 Oct 16:00Z+ are still counted when present.

- Non-8H months with at least one observed opening boundary-crossing settlement: 2974
- Non-8H months flagged cst_vs_utc_completeness_may_differ=true: 3122
- Non-8H CST 2026-11 rows (partial month, no month-end crossing in window): 212
- Opening boundary-crossing settlements counted one by one across non-8H months: 6109

## Also listed: already-known gaps

These are listed regardless of 8H/non-8H content.

- OL-USDT-SWAP 2025-10: official_rate_null (1/85 literal `None` in the official monthly ZIP); inventory complete=false.
- ZK-USDT-SWAP 2024-06: official_rate_null (1/54 literal `None` in the official monthly ZIP); inventory complete=false.
- RVN-USDT-SWAP 2026-11: pending_check / rest_51001_instrument_not_listed. Stays in the universe; no zero-fill. Recheck when the CST 2026-11 official monthly ZIP is published.
